| `/api/settings/automatic-whatsapp` | Settings → Templates (the Notification cards of Photo Selection Submitted, New Lead Welcome and Interview Welcome), behind `SETTINGS / Automatic WhatsApp Message` (`requireModule`: GET Read, PUT Read / Write). **Who every automatic message goes from** is `automatic-whatsapp/senderPolicy.js`, one `whatsapp_sender_policy` row a message (`ATTENDANCE_REPORT`, `WISHES`, `PHOTO_SELECTION_SUBMITTED`, `SHOOT_CREW_NOTICE`, `PAYMENT_REMINDER`, `INTERVIEW_WELCOME`) with a mode - `SAME`, "Sender": `sender_user_id` first, then every other active employee with Allow WhatsApp ticked in the dragged `FALLBACK` order; or `ROTATE`, "Rotation": every active employee with Allow WhatsApp ticked takes a turn, in the dragged `ROTATION` order (`whatsapp_sender_policy_member.list` keeps the two orders apart; anybody not in an order yet comes after it by name), each message from the next after `last_sender_user_id`, one who cannot passed over for the next. Each mode keeps its own setting. A send that may have gone stops there. Each message's own `PUT …/senders { mode, senderUserId, fallbackUserIds, userIds }` saves it under that message's existing guard. New Lead Welcome is not one of them - it goes from the lead's owner. **Photo Selection Submitted** ([automatic-whatsapp/photoSelection.js](src/modules/settings/automatic-whatsapp/photoSelection.js)): when a client submits their photo selection (`publication/service.js` `submitSelection`, told after `markSubmitted` has committed and never waited for), one message into a WhatsApp group the way the attendance report and the wishes go - `reportGroup.deliverToGroup`, from its own Sender or Rotation (`PHOTO_SELECTION_SUBMITTED`), each matched against their own groups by the name typed in Settings; the only group-capable path (Meta sends to one number). `GET /photo-selection` answers the switch, group, send order, last one sent and last error in the attendance panel's shape; `PUT /photo-selection/enabled { enabled }`, `/group { groupName }` (`""` clears), `/senders `{ mode, senderUserId, fallbackUserIds, userIds }``; `GET /photo-selection/senders/candidates` (Read / Write). Configuration is three columns on `hrm_settings` (`photo_selection_notify_enabled`, `_group_name`, `_last_error`, migration `20261020120000`) and its `whatsapp_sender_policy` row; the wording is message template `PHOTO_SELECTION_SUBMITTED` (`{client name}`, `{project name}`, `{event name}`, `{category name}`, `{selected count}`, `{submitted at}`), seeded by the same migration and edited behind `SETTINGS / Message Template`. **The queue is the activity log**: `afterSubmission` writes one `whatsapp_activity` row `QUEUED` keyed `isel-selection-submitted:<project>:<items>@<submitted instant>` - the instant `markSubmitted` stamped, so an item the studio reopens and the client sends again is a new submission with its own message, while a retry of the same write (refused with 409 before it is announced) writes nothing; the switch off or no group named writes nothing, and `startPhotoSelectionNotifier` (every minute in the API process, off with `PHOTO_SELECTION_NOTIFY_AUTOSEND=false`) works the queued rows through `deliverToGroup`, each try landing on the row (`requireClaim`: a claim the database refused is the pass skipped, never a send unrecorded); a failed one is retried on up to 3 passes, 5 minutes apart, within 2 hours - a pass tries every sender in turn and counts once (`listPending` groups tries closer than a minute) - and one still queued after 2 hours (the switch off meanwhile, the server down) is marked Failed "Not sent within two hours" rather than sent late (`expirePending`); never after a try WhatsApp did not answer or one a restart cut short mid-send (those are Resend's, from the Activity Log, which asks first). Nothing here resubmits a selection or touches the client's answer. **New Lead Welcome** ([automatic-whatsapp/newLeadWelcome.js](src/modules/settings/automatic-whatsapp/newLeadWelcome.js), `GET /lead-welcome`, `PUT /lead-welcome/enabled`): a lead the Google Sheet sync creates (`googleSheetsService.runGoogleSheetLinkSync`, after the row is written and recorded - never Add Lead, Bulk Entry, CSV import or the refill rebuild; a link's first import is welcomed like any other - never a number that is not 10-15 digits, never a row whose enquiry date is before yesterday; at most `MAX_PER_SYNC` 20 per pass, the rest logged Failed through `recordFailure`, and `MAX_PER_SENDER_HOUR` 30 per owner per hour, the rest a 429 retried later; the name passes through `safeName`) is queued as a `CRM_LEAD_WELCOME` activity row keyed by the lead, and the minute loop (`startLeadWelcomeSender`, off with `LEAD_WELCOME_AUTOSEND=false`) sends it through `sendMessageToLead` from the login of whoever the lead is assigned to (`leadNotifications.userIdForCrmName`), reading the lead again each pass. Refusals land on the row through `track`; retried three passes, five minutes apart, within two hours; then Failed and resendable from the Activity Log. Switch `hrm_settings.lead_welcome_enabled`, off by default; wording the lead's vertical's own template for the moment it arrived - `CRM_LEAD_WELCOME_V<verticalId>_OFFICE` / `_AFTER_HOURS` (`templateKeyFor`, office time from `officeSlot`); a lead with none (General, Team 33, no vertical, nothing written) is recorded Failed with why, not messaged. | **Interview Welcome** (`automatic-whatsapp/interviewWelcome.js`, started beside New Lead Welcome as `startInterviewWelcomeSender`): `GET /interview-welcome`, `PUT /interview-welcome/enabled` and `/sender `{ mode, senderUserId, fallbackUserIds, userIds }`` (Sender or Rotation, `whatsapp_sender_policy` `INTERVIEW_WELCOME`; in a Rotation, past one sender's hourly cap the next is asked), `GET /interview-welcome/senders/candidates` (Read / Write). Each candidate the interview sheet sync creates (`interview/sheetSyncService.js`) is queued as an `INTERVIEW_WELCOME` activity (recipient `PERSON`) and sent with `sendMessageToPerson` from that login - 20 per sync, 30 an hour. Columns `hrm_settings.interview_welcome_enabled` / `_sender_user_id` / `_last_error` (migration `20261029150000_interview_welcome_message`); `INTERVIEW_WELCOME_AUTOSEND=false` stops it. `test/interview-welcome.test.js`
| `/api/settings/certificates` | Settings → Employee → Certificate, an administrator's alone (`protect` then `isAdministrator` on the router - the Settings group is offered to administrators only, so no permission is granted role by role). `:kind` is `experience` or `employment` (anything else 404s), each with its own settings. `GET /:kind` reads that certificate's settings - `{ signatureUrl, note, closing, signatoryName, signatoryDesignation }` (`closing`: the line above the signature, "Sincerely," while empty), kept on `hrm_settings` - with `html`, the certificate's A4 document (heading, logo and head-office address from `payslipHeading`, as the payslip's; `services/experienceCertificate.js`); `PUT /:kind` saves `{ signature, note }` (the signature through `storeImage`, null removes it); `POST /:kind/preview` returns the document for unsaved edits and stores nothing. `:kind` `offer` is the Offer Letter's sign-off - the same settings less the note, in its own `offer_letter_*` columns (`SETTINGS_KINDS` in the controller; Employee Repository → Generate still knows only the two certificates), its `html` the offer letter template's A4 document (`services/offerLetter.js`; From is the studio's name and address, To and Employment Details blanks, filled per employee by `offerFromEmployee` from `OFFER_EMPLOYEE_SELECT` - name, address, email, first name, designation, department, work type, current work location, reports-to and date of joining - when a letter is generated) with the signature directly above the signatory's name (no signing line). `GET /offer-letter/timing` answers `{ generateAfterDays }`, how many days after joining each letter is generated (`hrm_settings.offer_letter_after_days`, null until set), and `PUT /offer-letter/timing` (strict, whole days 0-365) sets it. `GET /offer-letter/sections` answers `{ sections, employment, compensation }` (`employment`: which of section 1's lines print - `{ designation, department, employmentType, workLocation, reportingTo, dateOfJoining }` on `hrm_settings.offer_letter_employment`, all six until saved, `PUT /offer-letter/employment` saves it strict, and a section with no line on is left out; `compensation`: which of section 2's lines print - `{ monthlyGross, annualCtc, paymentDate, statutory, statutorySummary }` on `hrm_settings.offer_letter_compensation`, all four with the standard summary until saved; `PUT /offer-letter/compensation` saves it strict, a summary required while `statutory` is on; the salary lines read the employee's `totalSalary` and twelve of it, the payment date the payroll's `paybackDay`) - the letter's own `[{ title, format, content | points }]` after Employment Details and Compensation (PARAGRAPH: `content`; POINTS: `points`, printed as a bulleted list; NUMBERED: the same, numbered; a section saved with no format is a paragraph) (`hrm_settings.offer_letter_sections`), printed after the fixed sections in order - `POST /offer-letter/sections/import` (multipart `file`, one .docx of 5 MB at most, in memory) reads a Word document into `{ sections, warnings }` for review and saves nothing (a document's own numbering in front of a title is dropped); `POST /offer-letter/sections/preview` `{ sections }` draws the letter with them in place of the saved ones (`draftOfferLetterHtml`, checked as a save is, 40 at most) and saves nothing (`services/offerLetterImport.js`: `mammoth` to HTML, then headings and bold lines as titles, paragraphs, bulleted/numbered lists and table rows as the sections; refused unless it is a real zip with `word/document.xml`, and stopped once its parts inflate past 20 MB - counted as they inflate, never from the sizes the file declares), and `PUT /offer-letter/sections` `{ sections }` replaces them whole - in their printed order, with `{ builtin: "EMPLOYMENT" | "COMPENSATION" }` holding the two built-in sections' places, each once (`offerSectionOrder`; a list without them leads with both) (strict; a title up to 120, content up to 3,000 characters, up to 30 points of 500; 15 sections at most). |
| `/api/hrm/payslip-fields` | Settings → Payroll rules → Edit Payslip's Personal details and PF details: `GET /` (`?active=true` for those still offered) on `HRM / Payroll Register` or `HRM / Employee Management` Read; `POST / { section: PERSONAL|PF, label, inputType: TEXT|NUMBER|ALNUM|ANY|DATE|DROPDOWN, options? }` (`ALNUM` letters, numbers and spaces; `ANY` any character, symbols included) (`options` - at least two, up to 30 - a DROPDOWN's alone, stored in `payslip_field.options`; an answer must be one of them), `PUT /:id { label?, archived? }`, `DELETE /:id` (`{ cleared }`, its answers with it) on Payroll Register Read / Write, refused (403) to a caller limited to some branches, and a built-in payslip detail's name refused (400). Each employee's answers are `employee_payslip_value`, sent as `payslipValues { [fieldId]: value }` on `PUT /api/hrm/employees/:id` (each checked against its field's type; `""` clears) and returned on `GET /employees`; the payslip prints them after the built-in details, a PF one only where Template Settings carries PF. [modules/hrm/payslip-fields](src/modules/hrm/payslip-fields/service.js) `GET /built-in` lists the payslip's own items `{ group, label, name, deletable, shown, deleted }` - Salary, LOP Deduction, Salary Advance and Recovery always there and never deletable (`UNDELETABLE_ITEMS`), every other one deleted (off the list and every printout) until added with `deleted: false` and `PUT /built-in { label, shown?, deleted?, name? }` (Payroll Register Read / Write, every-branch reach) turns one on or off, deletes or restores it, or renames it - `name` is what every printout prints in its place (`renamedPayslipItems`, kept in `hrm_settings.payslip_built_in_items`; 409 where the payslip already has that name; its own label again puts the original back). |
| `/api/hrm/my-payslips` | HRM → Payslip, behind `HRM / My Payslip` (`requireModule`: Read). Answers for the signed-in login's own employee record alone (`user.linkedEmployeeId`; 404 where it has none) - nothing in the request names whose. `GET /` lists the months with a payroll row whose pay cycle has ended (`cycleGate`), newest first `{ year, month, label, periodFrom, periodTo, paymentStatus }`; `GET /:year/:month/pdf` prints that month's payslip from the register's own reading of the row (`listEntries` narrowed by `employeeId`) through `payslipPdfService`, laid out by Template Settings (`payslipHeading`, `payslipDetails`) - Net Pay the rounded salary as paid. The Personal Details lines (Emp Code to Bank A/c No.) print `employee.payslip_personal_details` as HR typed it on Edit Employee → Payslip Details (`payslipPersonalDetails`, checked by `employeeSchema`), never the rest of the record - "-" where nothing is typed. [modules/hrm/my-payslips](src/modules/hrm/my-payslips/service.js) |
| `/api/hrm/my-offer-letter` | My Profile → Offer Letter, for every employee by default - `protect` alone, no permission, by the studio's decision, since it answers for the caller's own record and nothing else. The signed-in login's own employee alone (`user.linkedEmployeeId`; 404 where it has none) - no id in the request. `GET /` answers `{ status, availableOn, fileName, html }`: READY once Settings → Offer Letter's days after joining have passed since the employee's `dateOfJoining` (India's calendar), PENDING with the day it comes, NOT_SET while Settings names no days, NO_JOINING_DATE with nothing to count from; `html` only when READY. `GET /pdf` prints it to an A4 PDF (`renderCertificatePdf`), 409 before it is ready. The letter is put together by `services/offerLetterDocument.js` - the same assembly Settings' preview uses - and filled from the record by `offerFromEmployee`. [modules/hrm/my-offer-letter](src/modules/hrm/my-offer-letter/controller.js) |
| `/api/hrm/payroll-register/payslip-template` | Settings → Payroll rules → Payslip template (below Edit Payslip), on the payroll register's router and so behind `HRM / Payroll Register` Read (handlers in `modules/settings/template-settings/controller.js`; Settings → Template Settings and `/api/settings/template-settings` are gone). `GET /payslip-template` answers the payslip heading `{ companyName, address, logoUrl, headOfficeName }`, taken live from Organization → Branding Logo (Studio Name, light Global Logo) and the Head Office branch's main address a line to a row (`services/payslipHeading.js`; "" where unset, `headOfficeName` null with no Head Office). `GET` also answers `details` - `PERSONAL` or `PERSONAL_AND_PF` (`hrm_settings.payslip_details`, both by default) - no longer chosen anywhere, it stays as saved; `GET /api/hrm/employees/payslip-details` answers the same to `HRM / Employee Management` Read for Edit Employee → Payslip Details. `payslip_company_name`, `payslip_address`, `payslip_logo_url` and `payslip_financial_year_start_month` on `hrm_settings` are retired and unread. `GET /payslip-template/sample.pdf` prints that heading over the sample payslip - Earnings beside Deductions with the month's amounts, no YTD, the PF details left off for `PERSONAL`; the app's default mark where no Global Logo is uploaded - to an A4 PDF through `services/payslipPdfService.js` (HTML written server-side, every value escaped, JavaScript off, the quotation PDF's browser). |
| `/api/sales`, `/api/sales/whatsapp-group-names` | Sales projects, group-name generator. A project's Status tab reads `GET /projects/:id/stage` (`PROJECT / Projects`, with the stage's Updates history) and writes the remark left after updating a stage with `POST /projects/:id/stage/updates` (`plansAProject` + `editsAStartedProject`; table `project_stage_update`). `POST /projects` takes an optional `clientLogin` `{ temporaryPassword }` (Create Project → Client Credentials): it needs `ADMIN / User Management` at Read & Write as well as the route's Sales Manager (403 otherwise), the password must be the shape the form generates, the **username is the client's mobile number** - the ten digits of the project's Client Main Number without the country code, worked out on the server (a `username` sent is ignored; no valid number is a 400) - and a number already used as a login username (a client booking again, most often) is a 409, and the account - `role` CLIENT, no IAM roles, no email (`user.email` NULL), `user.client_project_id` naming the project, bcrypt-hashed, not asked to change its password - is created in the project's own transaction. `GET /projects/:id/client-login` answers the project's login as `{ username, active }` or `null` (never the password) and `POST /projects/:id/client-login` `{ clientLogin }` makes one for a project booked without it (409 if it has one) - both guarded like Edit Sale Details plus User Management - for Edit Sale Details → Client Credentials. `POST /projects/:id/client-login/whatsapp` `{ message }` sends it from the caller's own WhatsApp to the project's client number (User Management, the WhatsApp send rate limit, 404 without a live client login). Deleting the project sets that account INACTIVE `POST /projects/bulk-status` (`ids`, `status`; `plansAProject`, then each project in reach and `mayEditStartedProject` - the rest skipped and counted) sets one Project Status on the ticked projects; only the projects are written, never a shoot. |
| `/api/project-management/pre-production` | The pre production board, one row per shoot. `GET /rows` lists rows (Super Admin sees all; other users see only shoots assigned to their linked employee), `GET /options` is the form's vocabulary (progress statuses and employees - travel mode moved to the production plan, and a travel key sent to `PATCH` is dropped), `PATCH /:shootId` saves one row (an optional `coverImage` - a data URI, the link the shoot already holds, or `null` to remove - is compressed to 600 KB / 2000px, stored in R2 under `projects/pre-production/covers`, and kept as `project_pre_production.cover_image_url`; the picture it replaces is deleted), and a project's follow-ups are added with `POST /projects/:projectId/followups` (`{ followupDate, remarks }`) and closed with `PATCH /projects/:projectId/followups/:followupId/done` — both answer with the project's follow-up summary. The General panel saves here too: `PATCH /projects/:projectId/general` (the two gifts and their Assigned To, Storage Needed, CRD Document and `applicabilityRemarks` only, through Edit Project's `saveStartProjectWork`; the **Production Manager** was once on this list and is now named on Edit Project with the other four seats, so Pre Production write no longer carries it), `PATCH /projects/:projectId/gifts/:gift/status`, and the hard disk's `GET /storage/options`, `GET /projects/:projectId/storage/:storageId` and `PATCH /projects/:projectId/storage/:storageId/status` — each reusing the Client Gifts or Client Storage handler, scoped by this router's permission, and a disk only when it is that project's. `GET /projects/:projectId/whatsapp-groups` and `PATCH /projects/:projectId/whatsapp-group` (`{ jid }`) link the project's WhatsApp group — only a jid in the caller's own `listGroups`, never a group another live project already holds. Guarded by `requireModule("PROJECT", "Pre Production")`, so reading needs READ and saving needs READ_WRITE. Rows are raised automatically by [pre-production/service.js](src/modules/project-management/pre-production/service.js) when a started project has a Pre Production Coordinator. **Import** of the studio's old pre production sheet: `GET /vertical/:vertical/import/template` and `POST /vertical/:vertical/import` (`:vertical` is `WED`, `QQ` or `CORP`, one template each), on top of the router's guard behind `checkPermission("SALES", "Sales Manager", "READ_WRITE")` because a row creates the booking and its shoot. One row per shoot: the project is found by the sheet's Event ID (`sales_project.sheet_event_id`) or created **under** it - the sheet's Event ID becomes the project's `event_id` - and the shoot likewise by `project_shoot.sheet_shoot_id`, taking it as its `shoot_id`; a row imported earlier under an app number is moved onto the sheet's ID when uploaded again, and an ID another row holds is refused by name. `scripts/renumberIdentifiers.js` leaves these rows alone. A row naming a project or shoot deleted in the app is refused unless the body carries `restore: true` (the dialog's "Restore deleted projects and shoots"), which undeletes it - the same row, so no duplicate - and updates it from the sheet; a project restores only under `mayDeleteProject` and only when it held no gallery, client login or client storage, a shoot only when no deliverable or post production was deleted with it, a people cell takes an Employee Code, a login or an employee's exact name (`services/importPeople.js`, shared with Production → Import) and anybody else - someone who has left - is kept by name with nothing linked, the old sheet's placeholders (WCR, N/A, IN OLD SHEET, #REF!...) come in empty, and the row's pre production is filled through `preProductionUpdateSchema`. The last columns of every template belong to the **project**, not the shoot beside it - one booking's rows repeat them. **Welcome Gift** and **Shoot Completed Gift** take Applicable / Not Applicable with an Assignee read like any other people cell, **Storage Needed** takes Yes / No, and **Project Validity (In Months)**, the three **Sales Pre / Production / Post Production Remarks** and the gifts go the same way: on a project already in the app only an answer it has not given is filled in, so the sheet never overrules the studio. A gift answer carries its tracking through the panel's own `applyGiftTracking` (Applicable with no status becomes Pending, which is what puts it on Client Gifts; Not Applicable clears the status, dates and assignee - and the sheet's assignee column is dropped for it, `giftablePeople`). Storage Needed = Yes raises the project's record through the register's own `ensureProjectClientStorage` (its Storage ID and its link to the earliest shoot are the register's), and **Received The Storage** = Yes then writes that record straight to `Delivered` - the end of the flow, skipping the eight moves the app never made, so it carries no hand-in, backup or transit detail - but only where the register would let a disc rest there: a project with a shoot still to come keeps its record at Yet to Receive (`shootStatusPendingFor`, the rule the status-change guard answers to). A record the studio has moved on or removed is left alone. **Date Created** is the sheet's day for the booking, written to `sales_project.created_at` (what the date-ordered lists read) on a project the file creates and on one it is linked to, since nothing in the app edits that column; left empty, the project keeps the date it has. A client typed into the app (same client name and vertical, exactly one) is not refused: it is kept and linked - it keeps its own `event_id` and is given the row's Event ID as `sheet_event_id`, and each row's shoot is linked to the shoot of that project with the same name - one of the name whatever the date, and where a booking holds several of a name (three "Rituals") the day picks one, else one with no date of its own, else the first unlinked one - so its rows pair with its shoots one for one and a row of an unknown day fills a blank rather than writing its day over one the app holds (`appShootFor`); a shoot with no match is added - keeping its own `shoot_id` beside the sheet's `sheet_shoot_id`, so Production and Post Production imports find it. The **project** keeps what the app holds and the sheet fills only what is empty; the **shoot** and its pre production answers are written from the sheet, which is the record of the shoot itself, so a date, place or timing it gives replaces the app's and only a cell it leaves empty is left alone; with `replace: true` a project that holds payments or a gallery, or that the caller may not delete, is linked the same way rather than skipped, and the response's `linked` lists the projects kept and why; a re-upload finishes what was left blank and edits nothing else: it never changes the group name, fills a client number or value only where empty, and writes to a shoot only what the sheet gave - never an empty cell over a value. A second upload of the same file updates rather than duplicates, reaches only projects the caller could edit (`projectAccessWhere` + the project's own branch), names people only where nobody is named, and never moves a status. At most 200 rows per request. `lenient: true` is the dialog's "Import rows with errors": a cell the schema or the row's own checks refuse is emptied and the row still imported (`parseLeniently` in [tableImport.js](src/services/tableImport.js), each pass dropping only the cells the schema named), the response's `emptied` naming the columns per row; a field the schema requires - Event ID, Client Name, Branch, Shoot ID, Shoot Name - cannot be emptied, so its row is still refused. Every people cell may be empty, CRM and Pre Production Coordinator included (their headings stay required); with no Pre Production Coordinator a project raises no pre production row, so the row imports and its pre production answers are not stored until one is named. `replace: true` deletes a project already in the app with the same client name (never one matched by number alone, nor one with payments or an Image Selection gallery, and only for a caller `mayDeleteProject` allows) through the Delete button's own `retireProjectWithin`, and imports the row in its place; `preview: true` works each row through and rolls it back, answering `replaced` and the refusals without changing anything. Columns and cell rules in [importColumns.js](src/modules/project-management/pre-production/importColumns.js), the write in [import.service.js](src/modules/project-management/pre-production/import.service.js), `test/pre-production-import.test.js` `PATCH /:shootId/status` sets a shoot's pre production status by hand - `WCR` (needs `followupDate`, added to the project's follow-ups in the same write), `HOLD` or `REJECTED` (need `remarks`), or `null` for automatic; kept in `project_pre_production.shoot_status*`. Rows (and the Projects list and a production shoot) carry `holds` - the departments holding one of the project's live shoots - from [services/projectHolds.js](src/services/projectHolds.js). |
| `/api/project-management/shoot-board` | The Shoot Board, `requireModule("PROJECT", "Shoot Board")`. `GET /shoots?from=YYYY-MM-DD&to=YYYY-MM-DD` (both required, at most 92 days) answers `{ shoots, freeShooters, unlinked }` - each card carries `team` and `crew` (`[{ service, person, assistants }]`), its project's `branchId` with the `branch` name read through the relation (the board's Branches filter; the row's own `sourceBranch` only where nothing is linked), and `freeShooters` is, for every day in the range, the shooters (`production/crew.js` skills) named on no shoot that date in any role, studio-wide ([shoot-board/freeShooters.js](src/modules/project-management/shoot-board/freeShooters.js)): the live shoots of started projects dated in the range on which the viewer or their reports (`dataScope.identityFor`) are named in any project or production role; every shoot for a Super Admin or `PROJECT / Show All Projects` (Read). `shoot-board/read.js`. |
| `/api/project-management/production` | The production board's data, one row per shoot of a started project with a Production Manager (each with `isMainEvent`, `startTime` / `endTime` and the project's `vertical`); the screen folds them into one row per project with its shoots as tiles (`frontend/src/lib/productionBoard.mjs`). `GET /shoots` (dataScope on branch plus the shoots the viewer's own name is on - as coordinator, manager, Production Team or service crew), `GET /shoots/:shootId` (with `canEditPlan` / `canRunSteps`, and `busyCrewEmployeeIds` - employees on another shoot's service crew at an overlapping time, by [production/availability.js](src/modules/project-management/production/availability.js): date + start to end, an end before the start being the next morning, back-to-back not a clash; saving the plan refuses a newly named crew member among them), `GET /options` (employees, each with `shooter` - holds one of the shooting skills in [production/crew.js](src/modules/project-management/production/crew.js) `SHOOTER_SKILLS`; saving the plan refuses a non-shooter newly named on a service - travel modes, Cameras-category assets, vehicle-category assets, active vendors; the shoot's services come with the shoot itself, read from its allocations, not picked), `GET /shoots/:shootId/activity`, `PATCH /shoots/:shootId/plan` (save or submit; the team is `teamEmployeeIds` plus `teamVendorIds` (active vendors); `crew` = who works each of the shoot's services per [production/crew.js](src/modules/project-management/production/crew.js) - a `LEAD` with a Cameras-category camera, and on a service named "Candid…" `ASSISTANT_1` / `ASSISTANT_2` without one, each an `employeeId` or an active `vendorId` (never both), keyed by service definition, a lead on every service needed to submit; travel per [production/travel.js](src/modules/project-management/production/travel.js) - Bus / Train / Flight / Own / Car / Office Vehicle / Other, with `vehicleNumberPlate` for Own / Car (trimmed, upper-cased, ≤20), `travelVehicleAssetId` for Office Vehicle (a live vehicle-category asset, checked only when newly named) and `travelModeOther` for Other; a save clears the detail the mode does not use, and submitting - or saving a submitted plan - needs it; `studioStartDate` (YYYY-MM-DD) and `studioStartTime` (HH:MM), when the crew leaves the studio, moved here from pre production's form - both needed to submit an outdoor plan and counted in `planCompleteness`), `PATCH /shoots/:shootId/seat` (one service's own person from the Production Schedule - `{ serviceDefinitionId, employeeId, vendorId }`, neither to empty it; every other seat is sent back as held and the save is `savePlan`'s, under its rules and its Production Manager check; `GET /shoots` rows carry `canEditPlan`, each service's person and the shoot's Assist for it), `POST /shoots/:shootId/copy-team` (`{ fromShootId }`, "Use the same team as above" on the Schedule: every service the shoot shares with that shoot of the **same project** (else 404) takes its person - an employee keeps the seat's cameras only where already on it, a vendor new to the seat starts Waiting - and every other service stays as it is; saved through `savePlan`, so its Production Manager check, shooters, active vendors, busy-at-that-time and one-employee-one-service refusals all hold - [plan.js](src/modules/project-management/production/plan.js) `copyTeam`), `POST /seat-swap` (`{ from, to }`, each `{ shootId, serviceDefinitionId }`: the two services' people swapped, on one shoot or across two the caller may both change, both rows locked in shoot-id order; an employee moved onto a shoot where they already work another service is a 400 - one employee works one service of a shoot (a service with "drone" in its name excepted, `crew.isDrone`), which every crew save holds (`crew.doubledPerson`, counted against the seats already held); an employee moved to a shoot they clash with is a 409 (a vendor is held to neither: several services of one shoot, and overlapping shoots, are allowed) with `details` naming the other shoot, its project and service only where the caller may change it - [swap.js](src/modules/project-management/production/swap.js)), `POST`/`DELETE /shoots/:shootId/files` (tickets: image/PDF via `documentStorage`, only while the saved travel mode is Bus / Train / Flight - 400 otherwise, re-checked inside the write - and kept, still removable, when the mode changes; photos via `imageStorage`; size, count and type limited), `PATCH /shoots/:shootId/footage` (**File in as it stands**: `{ footage }` with every answer but the service allowed to be blank, for the step's own people while the shoot is on `PHOTO_COPY`. A service's row holds its answers as they stand - what is typed is written, an empty box is written as `NULL` (`20261024150000_footage_answers_nullable` widened the five answers for this), and an entry blank in every box, remarks included, has its row removed. So reopening the step shows the card that was left, and a cleared box stays cleared; `saved` / `pending` in the answer count the services where all of `steps.FOOTAGE_ANSWERS` are filled, which is what completing the step waits on. Written as a clear-and-create per service (`clearFootage`, which handles the NULL service of a shoot with none, since `IN (NULL)` matches nothing), under a conditional update of the production row that locks it and refuses the save if the step moved on - a save racing a `completeStep` is a 409, not a row written into a finished shoot. Answers `{ data, saved, pending }` and logs `FOOTAGE_SAVED`. Completing the step then replaces whatever it holds), `POST /shoots/:shootId/steps/:step` (strict order through the seven steps of [production/steps.js](src/modules/project-management/production/steps.js) - four for an indoor shoot, which calls Shoot started "Ready"; the photo steps need their own photos - two for Happening now, one for the others; **Happy clients completes the shoot itself**, stamping `shoot_status` Completed and sending the Shoot completed message, with File in the one step after it; a paused shoot completes nothing; `PHOTO_COPY` takes `footage`, one entry per service booked on the shoot (or one with `serviceDefinitionId: null` when it has none), each with URL, backup employee, file count, size, first / last file name (required) and optional remarks, written to `project_production_footage`), `PATCH /shoots/:shootId/pause` (`{ paused, remarks }`: the shoot stopped where it stands, or started again - **not a step**, so nothing moves. Only from Shoot started being done until Happy clients ends the shoot (`steps.pauseRefusal`, `PAUSABLE_STEPS`), for the step's own people; the remarks - why - are required both ways, written to `shoot_status` / `shoot_status_remarks` with `SHOOT_STATUS_CHANGED` logged, conditionally on the status as read so two taps pause it once. No WhatsApp message. The retired shoot-status step's own route is gone: nobody sets On hold or Rejected, and Happy clients writes Completed), `GET /shoots/:shootId/files/:fileId/download` (a shoot's photo or ticket as an attachment, for anyone who can see the shoot; read only from our own bucket via `keyFromUrl`, or decoded from an inline data URI), `POST /shoots/:shootId/messages/:step/retry` (rate-limited per user; the Production Manager or a Super Admin, who are the ones shown whether a message went). Guarded by `requireModule("PROJECT", "Production")`; who edits is decided by being named (manager = `SalesProject.productionManagerEmployeeId`, the steps are run by anybody named on the shoot who also holds `PROJECT / Production Execution` Read / Write - the plan's `productionCoordinatorEmployeeId`, its Production Team, the employees of its service crew, and the manager; named without it, they read the steps; whoever completes the current step first completes it for all), Super Admin both, and so does `PROJECT / Show All Projects` at Read / Write (at Read it shows every shoot on the board). Rows are raised by [production/service.js](src/modules/project-management/production/service.js) beside `ensureProjectPreProduction`, carrying pre production's coordinator, travel answer and studio start over once (the deprecated `project_pre_production` coordinator and `travel_*` columns are dropped by one follow-up migration). Three steps post to the project's WhatsApp group from the linker's own WhatsApp; a failed send is recorded FAILED with a short safe reason and never blocks the step. **Import** of the studio's old production sheet: `GET /vertical/:vertical/import/template` and `POST /vertical/:vertical/import` (`WED`, `QQ` or `CORP`; the same columns for each, the vertical checked against every shoot), under the router's guard. One row per shoot, found by the sheet's Shoot ID that Pre Production → Import stored (`project_shoot.sheet_shoot_id`); it creates nothing, so the shoot and its production record must already exist. Refused unless the caller may change the plan on the board (`accessFor(...).canEditPlan`), with one answer for a missing and an unreachable shoot. Fills only what is empty - coordinator, team, travel, remarks, the photo copy (a **finished** one; a File in saved part way on the board is not an answer, so the sheet's replaces it, the pending whole-shoot row being cleared first) - submits the plan under the board's rules, and moves the step only forward, conditionally on the record being as read. Cells are read as Pre Production's are: placeholders come in empty, and a coordinator, team member or photo-copy backup is an Employee Code, a login or an exact name (`services/importPeople.js`); someone who has left is kept by name in `project_production.production_coordinator_name`, `project_production_team_member.employee_name` or `project_production_footage.backed_up_by_name` with nothing linked, and the board prints the name as written - a Save Plan leaves a name-only coordinator and team member in place (the plan form lists them read-only as "No longer with the studio"; choosing a real coordinator replaces the name, and a team member kept by name cannot yet be removed from the board). Needs the project to have a working Production Manager, or the shoot has no production record to fill. At most 200 rows per request. [importColumns.js](src/modules/project-management/production/importColumns.js), [import.service.js](src/modules/project-management/production/import.service.js), `test/production-import.test.js` |
| `/api/sales/quotations` | Version-wise quotations, grouped by enquiry. `GET` lists chains — the current version of each with its history attached — and `GET /:id` reads one version in full. `POST` raises a version (the first of a new chain, or the next of the chain that enquiry already has), `POST /:id/versions` raises the next version from the one named, `POST /:id/duplicate` copies a version onto another enquiry as a new chain (no longer called by the screen, whose Duplicate opens the generator on a copy and saves it through `POST`), and `POST /:id/link` re-enables that version's public link for another five days. `POST /:id/finalize` finalizes a version (`finalized_at` / `finalized_by_user_id`) - any generated version, newest or older - refused while another version of the quotation is finalized (409, "Another quotation version is already finalized…"; at most one per quotation, never unfinalized automatically, and MySQL's unique generated `finalized_chain_key` holds the same rule), while its link has lapsed (re-enable it first) and - when the generator sends `payload` - while what is on screen differs from what is saved. It locks the quotation's versions `FOR UPDATE`, writes only that version, raises no version, and its link stops expiring (`linkNeverExpires`, `linkExpiresAt: null`). A finalized version is read-only: no version is raised from it and it and its chain are not deleted (409). `POST /:id/unfinalize` changes only that version: kept exactly as it was (`unfinalized_at` / `unfinalized_by_user_id`), its link on a fresh five-day clock, no version raised. Both are idempotent (`unchanged: true`), read through the scope, and every row and version carries `finalization` - `state` `FINALIZED` or `UNFINALIZED`, the only two, with who and when - never on the client's copy. `POST /:id/versions` raises the next version from any unfinalized version, inside one transaction that locks the quotation's versions, so the same document sent twice at once raises one version (the second gets it back as `unchanged`) and a failed insert writes nothing. List versions also carry `createdByUserId` / `createdByName` and the finalizer's name. This is not the booking flag `finalized` below. `POST /drafts` saves a new quotation as a **draft** (`status: "Draft"`, version 01 of a new chain, no live link), `PUT /:id/draft` rewrites that same draft (refused with 409 once it is generated, or when `draftUpdatedAt` shows a newer save), and `POST /:id/generate` generates it in place under the same number. `POST /preview` is the generator's **Preview**: the unsaved form, parsed and built exactly as a save would (`quotationVersionColumns`), answered as the client's page receives a version (`clientFacingQuotation`, Quotation ID `Preview`, no link, no download, no expiry) - and written nowhere, so no row, number or version is ever drawn by previewing; every public route answers 404 for a draft, and the version, duplicate, link and PDF routes refuse one. `GET /:id/pdf` is the studio's own copy of a version as a file - the same render as the client's link, scoped like a change rather than like the picker's read, and deliberately *not* governed by the brand's **Allow Client Download**, which decides what the client's link hands out and not what the studio may save. There is no `PATCH`: a quotation already sent is never rewritten - only a draft, which nobody has been sent, is. Each version also carries `additionalNotes`, free text typed in the generator that prints on the client's copy above Terms & Conditions - held per version, so an earlier one shows the note it was sent with. Every version also carries `finalized` and `finalizedByEventId` — whether a project converted at that exact version, and which one — read from `sales_project.quotation_id`. Asked for one enquiry the list leaves drafts out; `withDrafts=1` lists them too, for Create Project's picker, which shows every version with its status and lets only a finalized one be chosen. A project is booked only at a version that is finalized now and raised for the project's own enquiry: `POST /api/sales/projects` (and a `PATCH` that moves a project onto a different version) checks the row `FOR UPDATE` and refuses a draft, a version never finalized or since unfinalized (409), another enquiry's version and one that does not exist (400), whatever the picker offered. A `PATCH` sending the version the project already holds keeps it unchecked, so unfinalizing a quotation never takes it off a project booked at it |
| `/api/sales/projects/payments-summary` | `GET ?ids=a,b,c` (at most 200, inside the proxy's header limit) - received, spent and still to receive for many projects at once, `{ [projectId]: { received, spent, toReceive } }`, for the project Boards' columns. The figures are a project's Payments tab's: cash entries Received and Spent, and every schedule part not Cleared (`services/projectMoney.js`, reading the scheduled payments' own `toPayment`). Under the same guards as `/projects/:id/payments` - `ACCOUNTS / Cash Management` Read and `PROJECT / Projects`, `Production` or `Pre Production` Read - and each id through `projectAccessWhere`: one outside the reader's reach is left out, never reported. |
| `/api/sales/projects/:id/sale-extras` | **Sale Extras** - Edit Sale Details' "Extra – Additional & Removed Items". Every shoot, service or deliverable **added**, **removed** or **re-scoped** (service: quantity, session timing, duration, work days, size; deliverable: quantity, variant, number of photos; a shoot has no scope) after a finalized quotation was pushed into the project. The baseline is `sale_baseline` on `project_shoot` / `project_post_production_allocation` / `project_shoot_deliverable` (migration `20261101160000_sale_extras`; every row that existed then is baseline): `createProject` fills it on the shoots the form marks `fromQuotation`, matched in order to the quotation's own shoots, and on only those of their lines the quotation itself lists under that shoot; linking a quotation on Edit Sale Details re-baselines the plan as it stands (`sale-extras/booking.js`). **Recording compares outcomes, not actions**: `reconcileSaleExtras` ([reconcile.js](src/modules/sales/sale-extras/reconcile.js)) diffs the rows against the baseline and the PUBLISHED lines under a lock on the project, writing `project_sale_extra` DRAFT lines - so a change made on any screen (Edit Sale Details, Edit / Start Project, imports, the client portal) is caught; undone changes cancel their drafts; a quoted service or deliverable deleted and the same item live on the same shoot again - recreated, picked again later, or restored - is the quoted item (its baseline moves to the live row) while the removal is unpublished; a REMOVED service or deliverable line is created at the value the item stood at (`details.source` QUOTATION - the quotation line's `unitPrice` x `quantity`, 0 if complimentary - or EXTRA - its published ADDED/MODIFIED amounts with their corrections; `details.value`), editable until publishing; rows an approved raised request wrote (`approvedRequestItems`: `project_change_request_item.applied_record_id`) are listed read-only as `status: "EXTRA_QUOTATION"` lines (`request-item-<id>`, with the extra quotation's code in `details.reference`) while live and unspoken-for, `GET` also returns `extraQuotationTotal` (approved extra quotations), the client's quotation link (`publicSaleExtrasFor` in [totals.js](src/modules/sales/sale-extras/totals.js)) lists each item once at its latest change by publish order (`clientItems`; the section does the same with `currentLines`) and lists those items and hand-raised approved extra quotations under Added Items (no amounts) and counts approved extra quotations in its `updatedTotal`, appearing once there is a published revision or an approved extra quotation, and such a row's removal is a REMOVED line with `details.source` EXTRA_QUOTATION at its approved amount; each line carries `action` - `REMOVE` on the newest line about a service or deliverable that is live, `ADD_BACK` on the newest about one that is off (`lineActions`); `POST /:extraId/remove` and `POST /:extraId/restore` (Remove / Add back; the guards Service Creation's own changes take, `plansAProject` + `editsAStartedProject`) take the row off (`removeServiceAllocationInTx` / `removeDeliverableAllocationInTx`, shared with Service Creation's delete) or undelete it - a quoted service with the workflow rows its removal closed and its automatic deliverables - and reconcile, refusing (409) a line whose action is no longer that, or an item already back; after a published removal the row comes back as an ADDED line prefilled with what the removal took off (`details.addedBack`); every shoot, service and deliverable is a line of its own with its own amount (a new shoot's services and deliverables, and what was removed with a shoot, too; a draft an earlier version had folded under another is made independent, a published one is never rewritten). `GET` (register readers; project reach) reconciles and returns lines, `draftTotal`, `publishedNet`, `updatedTotal`, revisions; `GET` also returns `extraShoots` (`extraShootsOf`: each shoot a line belongs to, with its `shootCode`, date, place and `status` ADDED / REMOVED / null, booked shoots first) and each line's `quantity`, related `shootCode` and `shootRef` (the shoot it is drawn under); `POST /publish { draftIds, reviewed, expectedRevision, lines: [{ id, amount, remarks }], published: [{ id, amount?, remarks? }] }` sits behind the guards `PATCH /projects/:id` uses (`answersForAProject` + `requireProjectEditor`); there is no separate save. In one locked transaction it checks the review, applies each waiting line's typed amount and remarks (`lines`; ₹0 is an amount, empty is unpriced and refuses the publish) and each published line's edit (`published`: a new amount becomes a CORRECTION line for the difference from the line's `effectiveAmount` - its amount plus its published corrections, `corrects_extra_id`, migration `20261101170000_sale_extra_corrections` - replacing any correction not yet published; remarks are kept on the row; remarks alone answer `{ remarksOnly: true }` with no revision), then publishes. Amounts are typed, pre-GST, signed by kind (added +, removed −, modified either); publish refuses a stale review, an unpriced line, a repeat or a race - keeping nothing - writes an immutable `project_sale_extra_revision` and logs `SALE_EXTRAS_PUBLISHED`; nothing is sent. A published line is never edited. Every write takes the project row lock first (reconcile, publish, a quotation re-link); unfinalize checks and writes under the version's lock, which publish re-checks. Not tracked without a finalized quotation. Published amounts add to `listProjectPayments` (`totalSaleExtras`, `totalSigned`), receipts' balance due, receipt messages, the client portal and the assistant, once each; the schedule, receipts and quotation are never written. A quotation carrying a revision cannot be unfinalized or swapped on the project (409). `GET /api/public/quotations/:token` gains `saleExtras` from the latest revision on that exact finalized version only ([totals.js](src/modules/sales/sale-extras/totals.js)): `added` / `removed` (`clientItems`: every published line once, corrections left out, each shoot's items together with the shoot first, each with `item`, `detail`, `category`, `relatedShoot`, `createdDate` = the line's `changedAt`), `updatedTotal`, `publishedAt`, `gstPercent` - no line amounts, net, revision number or ids. |
| `/api/image-selection` and `/api/image-selection/projects/:projectId/*` | Projects, folders, uploads, originals, gallery, publication, publish-share, deliverables, WhatsApp. A linked project is created by the list sync for every started Sales project (any status but Yet to Start; its name follows the WhatsApp group name), never chosen, and is listed only once one of its shoots has reached its day - from 00:00 India time on the shoot's own date, each shoot on its own, never by Production or Pre Production status (`projectsWithAShootReached` in `image-selection/shoot-visibility.js`, one batched read of the shoots' dates, which the dashboard counts through too) — held back, never deleted, until then; it is removed by `DELETE /api/sales/projects/:id` rather than from here — that delete soft-deletes every `ImageSelectionProject` holding the `salesProjectId`, both sections, inside the project's own transaction, then purges the Projects-page ones through `projects/service.purgeData()` once that transaction has committed (R2 cannot take part in it; a bucket failure therefore leaves a project already hidden, which the one-time script `scripts/one-off/purgeImageSelectionProjectsWithDeletedSalesProject.js` retries). Album Proofing rows are hidden and never purged: their proof pages carry `uploadId` values no foreign key protects. The list and the dashboard both apply `LIVE_PARENT` from `projects/repository.js`, so a linked project whose Sales project is deleted is out of the sidebar and out of every count even before it is purged. `GET /projects?attention=` narrows the list to one of the dashboard's views; `POST /projects/:id/gallery/selected/downloaded` is the browser's Download Selected reporting that it finished, the same stamp the originals copy-out writes. `GET …/whatsapp/groups`, `POST`/`DELETE …/whatsapp/group` and `POST …/whatsapp/send-group` are the project's WhatsApp group: linked only to a group the caller's own paired WhatsApp answered for, one live project per group, and the invite sent into it from whoever pressed Send — on their own number — once per publish version `POST .../gallery/selected/downloaded { asOf, folderId? }` records a Download Selected run: with `folderId`, only the submitted items whose every pick sits in that folder are stamped (`gallery/repository.coveredDeliverableConfigIds`), without it every submitted item; the desktop copy (`originals/service.copySelected`) stamps its one deliverable or all. The project's own stamp moves only once every submitted item is up to date (`stampSelectedDownloaded`). |
| `/api/image-selection/projects/:projectId/folders` | The folder tree ([folders/](src/modules/image-selection/folders/)), `requireModule("IMAGE_SELECTION", "Project Management")`. `GET /` lists the tree (and grows a Project Management shoot's folder on its day - a hand-made root folder of the shoot's name is **linked to the shoot by id** once, `projectShootId`, never matched by name again). `POST /` creates, `PATCH /:folderId { name }` renames **the same row** - same id, parent, photos; on a manual project the typed selection items filed under it (`image_selection_deliverable_config.manual_shoot_folder_id`) follow by id and their `manual_shoot_name` copy is rewritten in the same transaction; the link is `ON DELETE CASCADE`, so no item outlives its folder, and an item under a shoot name no folder has is refused (`POST …/deliverables` 404). **`DELETE /:folderId` deletes permanently** (`?acknowledgeSubmitted=true` answers the 409 over client-submitted selections): the folder and its subtree, their photo rows and picks, and on a manual project the items (and limits) filed under them, in one transaction; R2 previews and thumbnails are purged first, except any key another photo row still carries (`keysUsedElsewhere`); originals on the studio's storage are never touched; the publication - token, PIN, short code - is not changed, so the client link keeps serving every other folder. A Project Management shoot folder is renamed and deleted in Project Management only (409 here); Project Management deleting a shoot takes its folder the same way (`removeForShoot`). |
| `/api/image-selection/projects/:projectId/uploads` | The upload dock ([uploads/](src/modules/image-selection/uploads/)), `IMAGE_SELECTION / Project Management` (Album Proofing re-mounts `createUploadsRouter` under its own grant): `GET /`, `GET /:uploadId`, `DELETE /:uploadId` (a legacy PENDING/UPLOADING row), the batches (`POST /batches`, `GET /batches/:batchId`, `/retry-failed`, `/events` SSE) and `POST /derivatives` - the one way a photo arrives: the compressed preview and thumbnail a client built, plus the photo's details and, from the desktop app, `checksum` (BLAKE3 of the original, stored as `source_checksum`; finishing a desktop-registered row also repairs a size registered as 0). **No original reaches this server in any form**: the chunked uploader (`POST /initiate`, `PUT /:uploadId/chunks/:chunkIndex`, `POST /:uploadId/complete`) is retired and those paths answer `410 { message }` naming what replaced them; the disk staging (`.incoming`, `.chunks`) is swept once at boot ([uploads/service.js](src/modules/image-selection/uploads/service.js) `sweepLegacyChunkDirectories`), and `/uploads/image-selection/*` is never served by the static `/uploads` route ([middleware/privateUploadRoot.js](src/middleware/privateUploadRoot.js) answers 404 for every spelling of that path - case, encoding, backslashes, dot steps, Windows' trailing dots and spaces - the rest of `/uploads` - sales attachments - unchanged). The sweep only runs under a root whose last folder is `image-selection` and that is not a filesystem root, so a mis-set `IMAGE_SELECTION_UPLOAD_ROOT` deletes nothing |
| `/api/image-selection/album-proofs`, `/api/image-selection/album-proofs/projects` and `/api/image-selection/album-proofs/projects/:projectId/uploads` | Album Proofing: its own projects (list, New Project from a saved group name or a manual name, edit, archive, delete), albums, versions, QC rounds, corrections, client publication, and the upload dock mounted again under its own grant. A correction round's next version is created by `POST /:albumId/revised-version` — the corrected spreads landing — and only then submitted by `resubmit-qc`; resolving a correction creates nothing ([revisionGate.js](src/modules/image-selection/album-proofs/revisionGate.js)). Guarded by `IMAGE_SELECTION / Album Proofing` (reads also by `Album Proofing QC`, which alone rules QC). Never through `projects/` — the two modules share a table, not code |
| `/api/image-selection/signature-albums` | The Signature Album, studio side ([signature-album/](src/modules/image-selection/signature-album/)): `GET`/`POST /projects/:projectId` (the project's album, created from the studio-wide defaults), `GET /projects/:projectId/sources/folders` and `/sources/images` (what the picker may add - the project's folders with READY counts and its READY photos, marked when already in the album), `GET`/`POST /:albumId/photos`, `DELETE /:albumId/photos/:photoId`, `GET /:albumId/photos/ids?search=` (every id the gallery's search matches, for Select All) and `POST /:albumId/photos/remove` `{ photoIds }` (bulk delete, up to 2000: all of them must be this album's or none is touched, files purged before any row, a cloud failure deletes nothing) (the album's own uploads: the thumbnail and preview the browser built, magic-byte checked and put in R2 under `image-selection/signature-album/`, never an `image_selection_upload`; each is written with its item in one transaction, in the album's first chapter - "Gallery", made under a per-album lock by the first upload of an album with none - so it is in the client's album as it lands; `GET` takes `search` and `order`; deleting one - or the album, or its project - purges its R2 objects first), `PATCH /:albumId` (title, subtitle, date, cover, alignment, ending - the cover is `coverAlbumPhotoId`, one of this album's own uploads, which clears a project-photo `coverImageId`; an older album's project-photo cover keeps drawing until replaced, and publishing accepts either - plus the four cover placement values Adjust Cover and the Design tab's drag both write), `/:albumId/design` (theme, cover style, layout, spacing, colours, fonts, and the logo variant - Auto / Dark / Light - that picks which Global Logo the album's pages draw; Auto reads the album's effective background, so a dark album gets the dark-mode mark and a light one the light-mode mark, decided once in `signatureAlbum.helpers.js` for the client page and the studio's preview alike), `/:albumId/access` (Require PIN, expiry, Allow Download and Allow Social Share), `DELETE /:albumId` (the album's own rows only), chapters (`POST`, `PATCH`, `DELETE`, `PATCH /chapters/reorder`), items (`POST /items` from `SELECTED` / `FOLDER` / `MANUAL`, `DELETE /items/:itemId`, `POST /items/remove`, `PATCH /items/reorder`, `PATCH /items/:itemId` to move or star), `GET /highlights` and `PATCH /highlights/reorder`, `GET /:albumId/preview` and `/preview/chapters/:chapterId/items` (the client's content from the client's own builder, for a draft as much as a published album, with photo URLs behind this guard - what the workspace's preview draws), `POST /publish` (refused with the list of what is missing), `/unpublish`, `/share-link` (new code and token), `/reset-pin`, `GET /share` (link, PIN, expiry, message, wa.me link, QR of the link alone) and `POST /share/whatsapp-group` (the linked group, from the caller's own WhatsApp). Guarded by `IMAGE_SELECTION / Signature Album`; `GET`/`PATCH /settings` - the studio-wide defaults - by `SETTINGS / Organization`. Mounted ahead of the project router. Every photo is a reference to an `image_selection_upload` row; the module never writes that table, the project or the gallery publication |

Registering photos that stay on the studio's drive or NAS, and copying the client's selections out to the Selected Output Path. }`: PIN off clears the hash so the guest join stops asking, on keeps or makes one, `newPin` draws a different PIN, `newCode` a fresh unique code (a collision is drawn again); a typed code or PIN is refused; an empty request changes nothing; never logged; 409 for a revoked or missing link; Read / Write), `GET .../share/poster` (the QR Template's kit for an ACTIVE, unexpired link - 409 otherwise: the URL, short code and PIN the Share dialog already shows, the event's name, the QR as its module grid at error correction H from the same `qrcode` call, and the studio's Global Logo light/dark as data URLs read from our own bucket by key, under `image-selection/branding/` only, ≤ 2 MB, else null so the client uses its fallback mark - [share/poster.js](src/modules/ai-feature/share/poster.js)), `GET .../detection` (that event's Detection & Storage for its Project Dashboard - the dashboard panel's own repository reads, each given the event id, so no figure is a studio total; 404 for a deleted event), people, matches, share, activity. Only the studio's own upload is read: a `data:` URI or a file in this server's public bucket, read by key; a favicon given as any other address is never fetched. With no favicon, or one it cannot read or draw, it redirects to the bundled PNG of the same name at `FRONTEND_URL/icons/` |

Photo Selection's `DELETE /projects/:id` likewise only clears its tick while module records remain. Read-only summaries over live, unarchived Projects-page projects: culling counts the latest run per shoot scope, editing groups `image_selection_edit` by project and state in the database (no photo row loaded), the album reads each status through `signatureAlbum.helpers` `publishProblems` / `deriveStatus` - the tab's own rule. The activity series covers that same range - one range control on the page, not two - and only how finely it is drawn (a bar a day, a week or a month) is derived, from the span; Lifetime starts at the first pick a client ever made rather than at 1970. Every stage is the Projects sidebar's own rung ([dashboard/classify.js](src/modules/image-selection/dashboard/classify.js) is the one mapping, and the projects list reads it too for `?attention=`); counts are grouped in the database, no photo row is loaded, no folder is scanned and no bucket is listed. Each recent project carries `readyToDownloadFolderId`: on a Yet to Download row, the first root folder in the tree's order standing for a shoot whose submitted selection is still to be downloaded (`readyToDownloadConfigs` / `downloadFolderOf` in classify.js, matched the way the project page scopes Download Selected); null on every other row and where no folder stands for that shoot. The root folders are read for those rows only. Guarded by IMAGE_SELECTION → Project Management (`READ`). Mounted ahead of the project router like settings **Per selection item since 20 Oct 2026:** each item carries its own download stamp (`image_selection_deliverable_config.selected_downloaded_at`), so Yet to Download lists exactly the submitted items not yet copied out since they were sent (`classify.readyToDownloadConfigs`), Downloaded means every submitted item is up to date, and `clientSubmitted` - the Client Submitted card and its list - is "any item submitted" (the gallery's own stamp for a gallery without items); the Status stage is unchanged. Each row carries `submittedItems` (`{ id, name, selectedCount, submittedAt, downloadedAt }`). **Expand:** every recent project carries `expandEnabled` - false once the project is disabled (`image_selection_project.disabled_at`, one state for everyone); `?expand=true` counts every card and period figure by the same rules over only the enabled projects while `recentProjects` still lists every project (storage is null there). `PUT /expand/:projectId { enabled }` (Project Management `READ_WRITE`, under the module guard) sets or clears `disabled_at` and `disabled_by_user_id` without touching `updated_at` ([dashboard/expand.js](src/modules/image-selection/dashboard/expand.js)); the id must be a Projects-page project (404 otherwise). While a project is disabled, [projectEnabled.js](src/modules/image-selection/projectEnabled.js) `requireProjectEnabled` answers 409 `PROJECT_DISABLED` ("This project is disabled. Enable it from Expand to upload photos or send the client link.") on `uploads` `POST /batches`, `/batches/:id/retry-failed`, `/derivatives`; `originals` `POST /register`, `/register-files`, `/register-batch`; `publication` `POST /` and `/regenerate-pin`; `publish-share` `POST /prepare`; `whatsapp` `POST /send`, `/send-group`, `/notify-app`; and AI Culling's `send-to-photo-selection`. Unpublishing, cancelling an upload, folders, culling and editing stay open. Its published link is paused in `publication/service.authorize` - every public gallery route answers 404 "This gallery is temporarily unavailable." - with the publication row (URL, PIN, expiry) untouched, so the same link opens again once enabled. The Projects list returns `disabledAt` for the sidebar's Disabled mark. |

| `/api/image-selection/projects/:projectId/ai-culling` | AI Photo Culling ([ai-culling/](src/modules/image-selection/ai-culling/)), `protect` + `requireModule("IMAGE_SELECTION", "Project Management")`: `GET /?folderId=` (the scope's latest session, ready-photo count, `photoCounts` — `{ total, ready, processing, uploading, failed }` from the scope's uploads, where uploading also counts files an UPLOADING batch announced but has not registered — `confidence` — `{ high, medium, low, average }` for a settled run, bands from `constants.CONFIDENCE_LINES` — `engines` — `{ faces, scene }`, whether a run started now would use each optional engine — facet counts, `newPhotoCount`, `scenes` — `[{ id, label, count }]` in profile order, `coverage` — `{ moments: { total, kept, missing }, keyPeople: { total, kept } }` or null), `POST /sessions` (`mode`, `folderId`, `targetCount`; 409 when an active run's scope overlaps; `awaitUploads` + `expectedCount` make a **run that begins with the import** - created while the photos are still arriving, sized by what the import announced, `awaits_uploads` on the row, never queued at creation: the desktop analyzes each photo as its preview lands through `pending` / `signals`, and the run is handed over only once nothing in its scope is still uploading or processing - `POST /sessions/:id/run` answers 409 `importInProgress` with the in-flight counts until then, or `{ force: true }` takes it past them; at hand-over the target is re-checked against the photos that landed (422 `targetUnreachable` with both numbers, the run keeps waiting) and `totalImages` becomes that count; a server-analyzed import run is handed over by the last landing photo itself (`importHook.photoLanded`, told by the derivatives route, coalesced per project), a desktop-analyzed one by the workstation when its drain is done; `PATCH /sessions/:id` `{ mode, targetCount }` changes the mode of any run still waiting; the overview carries `importing` - `{ analyzed, inFlight, ready, settled }` - while such a run waits; boot recovery leaves these runs alone like a waiting desktop run), `GET /sessions/:id`, `GET /sessions/:id/events` (SSE), `GET /sessions/:id/results?filter&sort&order&page&scene` (filters include `accidental` and `cut_faces`; `scene` is a profile id), `PATCH /sessions/:id/results/:resultId/decision` and `POST /sessions/:id/decisions` (`decision: null` clears an override; ids deduped), `GET /sessions/:id/groups[/:groupId]` (`type` DUPLICATE, BURST or SIMILAR; members best first, each with `rank`) and `POST .../groups/:groupId/best` (the chosen frame becomes rank 1), `POST /sessions/:id/finalize` and `/reopen` (`{ acknowledge }` after a 409 with `requiresAcknowledgement`), `POST /sessions/:id/cancel`. The caller's own learned preferences: `GET /preferences` (features of kind `code`, `scene` or `context` — a code learned in one scene — and `groupTaste` `{ choices, weights }` or null), `PATCH /preferences` (`{ enabled }`, strict) and `POST /preferences/reset` — the user always comes from the token. People and roles for the subject stage: `GET /people` (ranked clusters with cover face, photo count and `role`) and `PATCH /people/:personId` (`{ role }` from the four roles or null, 404 outside the project; kept only for older desktop builds - the screens no longer set roles and culling does not read them). The desktop hand-off for a run started with `localAnalysis: true` (`analysis_source` DESKTOP, waiting unqueued): `GET /sessions/:id/pending?cursor&take` (READY photos in scope without technical signals, with `originalPath` + `storageRootId` and `facesIndexed` — whether the people index already holds the photo, so the desktop does not re-post its faces) and `POST /sessions/:id/signals` (`{ engine: { name, version, preprocessVersion? }, items: [{ uploadId, technical?: { blurScore, exposureScore, technicalScore, perceptualHash, structureSignature?, reasonCodes }, faces?: { faceCount, faceQualityScore, eyesOpenScore, expressionScore, reasonCodes, details?: [{ box: { x, y, w, h }, confidence }] } }] }`, ≤200 items, enum reason codes, out-of-scope ids skipped by name; an item may instead carry `localSource` - `LOCAL_SOURCE_UNAVAILABLE`, `LOCAL_SOURCE_CHANGED` or `LOCAL_SOURCE_UNREADABLE`, the workstation could not use the original - which stamps both stages with `ANALYSIS_FAILED` + that code so no server stage, the scene stage included, reads the photo's preview in the original's place; `pending` items also answer `sizeBytes` (a stored 0 as `null`, size unknown) and `sourceChecksum`, which the workstation verifies the file against before analyzing it) sit behind `DESKTOP_AI_ENABLED`; `POST /sessions/:id/run` (any app) hands the run to the worker, which seeds the rest, reads previews only for unstamped photos, groups and ranks. Decisions and best-of-group changes carry an optional `source` (`KEY`, `CLICK`, `PREVIEW_PANEL`, `BULK`, `COMPARE_DIALOG`; default `UNKNOWN`; a bulk call of more than one photo is always `BULK`) and `platform` (`WEB` | `DESKTOP`; default `WEB`, which the desktop app always overrides), the overview's `photoCounts.held` counts the photos imported for culling and still waiting (there is no route past the cull: finalizing moves the imported Keeps into AI Editing, `editing_hold_at`, and only a finalized run's Keeps - `POST /sessions/:id/send-to-photo-selection` below - reach Photo Selection; the AI Editing tab has no send there); and `POST /sessions/:id/views` (`{ resultIds ≤200, source: GRID | PREVIEW, platform? }`, strict) records which photos were seen during review, once per photo, source and person, only while `REVIEW_READY` - both are training-label quality only and change no decision. Every route 404s for a soft-deleted project |
| `/api/image-selection/culling-learning` | The studio's culling-training consent and the model registry's read-only list ([culling-learning/](src/modules/image-selection/culling-learning/)), `protect` + `requireModule("SETTINGS", "Organization")`: `GET /consent` → `{ consent, consentAt, consentBy }`, `PATCH /consent` `{ consent }` (strict boolean), `GET /models` |
| `/api/image-selection/projects/:projectId/faces` | The People filter's staff side: `GET /status` (how far the project's photos have got - unscanned / waiting / processing / completed / failed - how many people were found, and whether the engine can run at all) and `POST /queue` (hand the worker every READY photo it has not seen or gave up on; answers 202 at once, the work is in the background). Guarded by `IMAGE_SELECTION / Project Management`, the permission of the gallery these people are found in; 409 when the filter is switched off. No screen calls these yet - they exist so a gallery that predates the filter can be processed. `POST /ingest` is the Virtual Curation desktop's hand-off of one photo's faces (see [Desktop Virtual Curation hand-off](#desktop-virtual-curation-hand-off)); a 404 unless both desktop switches are on |
| `/api/desktop` | The Virtual Curation desktop app's first call: `GET /capabilities` - which desktop extras this server has on, the face model it expects and its dimension, the People filter's switch and thresholds, and whether deferral and the managed runtime are offered; no row of any table. `GET /runtime/manifest`, `GET /runtime/models/:file`, `GET /runtime/face-engine.py` and `GET /runtime/edit-engine.py` serve the desktop's managed Local AI runtime - the face engine script, AI Editing's engine script (named in the manifest's `editEngine` when this server has it) and the two open-source model files this server itself runs, with their digests. Behind `protect` alone (any signed-in user), and a 404 unless `DESKTOP_VIRTUAL_CURATION_ENABLED=true`. See [Desktop Virtual Curation hand-off](#desktop-virtual-curation-hand-off) |
| `/api/image-selection/whatsapp` | The coordinator's own WhatsApp as Image Selection sees it — `GET /session`, `POST /session/connect` — and `POST /link-all`, which links every unlinked project to the group carrying its name and sends nothing. The same per-person session the CRM chat pairs, read here behind `IMAGE_SELECTION / Project Management` because a post-production coordinator need not hold `SALES / CRM Leads`, and a screen may not be given a finer permission than the endpoint it reads |
| `/api/sales/quotation-settings` | The rates the Quotation Generator prices with, in one row: service rate, deliverable rate, default discount (stored and edited, but no longer pre-filled into a new quotation, which starts at 0%), GST. `GET` to read, `PUT` to save - either on `SALES / Quotation Management` or on `ADMIN / System Settings`, since Settings → Quotation Settings → General is now where they are edited. A brand's own prices, GST and discount (`/api/sales/quotation-brands`) take precedence where set. A quotation stamps these into its own payload as it is generated, so changing them never reprices one already sent |
| `/api/sales/quotation-brands` | The three quotation brands and what each offers. `PATCH /admin/:code` also carries `allowClientDownload`, the brand's Yes/No on whether its clients may save the quotation as a file - see `/api/public/quotations`. `GET /` lists them (`?includeInactive=1` for Settings), `GET /:code/configuration` is the brand's services, deliverables, packages, terms, merged PDF wording and pictures - what the Quotation Generator loads when a brand is picked - and both answer for `SALES / Quotation Management` READ or `ADMIN / System Settings` READ. Everything under `/admin` is Settings → Quotation Settings and answers for `ADMIN / System Settings` (`requireModule`: GET at READ, the rest at READ_WRITE). `GET`/`PATCH /admin/general` is the studio's shared content (the shared words and the map, collages and timeline), routed ahead of `/:code`. Per brand: `PATCH` the brand (name, its own PDF wording overrides - the shared keys are dropped - GST, default discount, active, and `pdfAssets` - `coverSets`, the cover and Thank You photographs per group of shoots; every picture arrives as a data URI and is stored through `storeImage`/`storeImages` exactly as the Global Logo is, so the column holds links). `GET /:code/configuration` also returns `shoots`, the shoots that brand quotes for and the only ones a set may name, `POST`/`PATCH` a service, deliverable, package or term, `PUT .../order` to reorder, `DELETE` a package or term. Services and deliverables are hidden rather than deleted, because a quotation may still name them. Nothing here rewrites a version already raised `GET /:code/shoots` answers a brand's shoot names alone, for CRM → Add Lead and the lead popup (each Event Details row offers the lead's own vertical's shoots), and is readable with `SALES / CRM Leads` Read as well as the two reads of `/:code/configuration` - the priced configuration itself is not opened to CRM Leads |
| `/api/sales/callyzer` | The Callyzer **Call Log API**, proxied. `GET /status` says whether credentials are configured; `POST /reports/:report` runs one of `summary`, `employee-summary`, `analysis`, `never-attended`, `not-pickup-by-client`, `unique-clients`. Their documented rules are checked here first, so a bad range is one round trip rather than two. The browser never calls Callyzer directly - the AccessKey stays on the server |
| `/api/sales/whatsapp-chat/*` | Every route that names a lead validates the id through `IDENTIFIER_PATTERN` from [services/identifiers.js](src/services/identifiers.js) rather than a pattern of its own, and so does [services/crmMediaStore.js](src/services/crmMediaStore.js) when it builds an object key. Both had `/^DBS-[A-Z]{2}-EID-\d{1,12}$/` written out by hand — the shape an enquiry id had *before* it carried its vertical and branch (`DBS-DD-EID-4867`) — so both refused every id minted since (`DBS-EID-DD-HYD-000024`): WhatsApp Chat answered "That is not a valid CRM lead id." on every lead, and the media store had nowhere to file a single attachment. The guard is deliberately loose about *which* scheme an id belongs to: its job is that the value is safe to put in a query, a file name and a socket, and whether it names a lead this caller may read is the handler's question, asked against the lead |
| `/api/sales/whatsapp-chat/session` | What this account's WhatsApp is doing, plus the four things that decide whether the application is held shut until it is linked. **All four have to say yes before anybody sees a QR**, and only two of them are the server's: `linkRequired` is `whatsapp_required` on the account (Employee Preference → WhatsApp → **Allow WhatsApp**, off until somebody ticks it — and the tick alone also lets them pair through `POST /session/connect`, which otherwise needs Read on `SALES / CRM Leads`, `HRM / Interview` or `HRM / Employee Management`) *and* not an administrator (a Super Admin, or any **active** role carrying the Administrator tick — never held, because they do not work leads and they are the people who have to reach Settings to put somebody else's WhatsApp right). The other two are the switches the browser applies in `whatsappLinkNeeded`: the studio's (Settings → User Settings → WhatsApp, off lifts the gate for everyone) and this account's own. A gate that decides not to hold you renders **nothing**, so from the browser a missed tick, a switched-off studio and an administrator's exemption all look identical — which is why "it never asks me for the QR" is not a question the dialog can answer. And there is a fifth thing, which is not a condition but is usually the answer: **an account that is already linked is not asked to link**, which is the gate doing its job and looks identical to all four failing. Whether an account has paired outlives the process — the credentials are on disk under `WHATSAPP_SESSION_DIR`, which is what `restoreSessions` brings back at boot — so it can be answered from outside a running server, unlike the live status. `npm run report:whatsapp-gate -- <name>` prints all five for an account and names the first one that said no ([scripts/reportWhatsappGate.js](scripts/reportWhatsappGate.js)); reads only, safe against production |
| `/api/sales/whatsapp-chat/my-session` | The same answer, about **the caller's own** account, mounted **above** the router's `SALES / CRM Leads` guard. HR offering to send an onboarding invite or a new joiner's login from their own paired WhatsApp has to know whether they have one — and they hold `HRM / Employee Management`, not CRM Leads, so behind that guard the answer was a 403 and the button could not be drawn honestly. It returns the caller's link state and the two switches over it and nothing else: no lead, no thread, no message, nobody else's session, so there is nothing here for CRM Leads to guard |
| `POST /api/hrm/onboarding-invites/:id/send-whatsapp`, `POST /api/hrm/employees/:id/send-credentials-whatsapp`, `POST /api/hrm/interview/invites/:id/send-whatsapp` | The onboarding invite, and a new joiner's login, handed over on WhatsApp from the sender's own account instead of by email. The **wording** is posted from the dialog's editable box, because that box is the point — somebody adds a line, or writes it in the candidate's language — so a server that rebuilt the message would discard what they typed.
